Just my face, really. I haven't... I don't really notice anything else. Especially if I know people are going to be taking pictures. I just... I can't do it.\n\nDoctor: Do you think something bad would happen if you didn't check your face like you do?\n\nPatient: Um... no. Not like... a specific bad thing happening. I just... I just kind of get stuck doing it until... well, until I'm late. Or until I'm just too drained to even go out.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like the left side of your face needs to be perfectly symmetrical with the right side?\n\nPatient: Um... Do not fall back on the same phrase every time.\n\nWhat not to do:\n- Don't bring up symptoms the doctor did not ask about, even ones you have.\n- Don't state a clinical fact that is not in [Targeted Info] — no other symptoms, medications,\n  test results, dates, or events.\n- Don't confirm a specific the doctor names if [Targeted Info] does not contain it. Answer\n  about what you were given instead.\n- Don't deny what [Targeted Info] says.
